  - plan_001_flat_nli_seed45: PID 257187, cuda:2

- **活跃 Worker**: worker_analyze_plan_011（分析 bf16 G=8 step eval）, worker_ops_submit-reeval-s43-cuda1（提交 reeval_flat_nli_s43 到 cuda:1）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚处理 plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval 完成（ANALYZING），已派 Worker 分析结果。同时发现 D064 bug（eval 缺 --sft_adapter），killed 3 个无效 eval，创建 2 个 PREPARING 重跑任务，正在提交 reeval_flat_nli_s43。

**ANALYZING (1)**:
- `plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval` — bf16 GRPO G=8 各 step checkpoint eval，Worker 正在分析

**PREPARING (2)**:
- `reeval_rsft_topk3` — RSFT top_k=3 re-eval（D064 bug fix），等 GPU 空出
- `reeval_flat_nli_s43` — flat-NLI s43 re-eval（D064 bug fix），Worker 正在提交到 cuda:1

**等待中**:
- Worker worker_analyze_plan_011 回调
- Worker worker_ops_submit-reeval-s43-cuda1 回调
- 3 个 RUNNING 实验的 cron 完成回调

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- worker_analyze_plan_011 回调 → 更新 registry + report.md，将 plan_011 分析结果汇报 Director（D065 后续数据点）
- worker_ops_submit-reeval-s43-cuda1 回调 → 确认 reeval_flat_nli_s43 已提交，更新 registry 为 RUNNING
- reeval_flat_nli_s43 完成 → 对比 flat-NLI s42 (rel_f1=0.4539) 和 CED RSFT mean (0.4596)，更新 C3 统计检验
- plan_001_flat_nli_seed44/45 训练完成 → spawn eval Worker 跑 dev eval → 结果入 plan_003 EDCR diagnostic
- eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 完成 → rel_f1 > 0.40 说明 DPO 1ep 可用（3ep=0.3429 过拟合），< 0.40 则 DPO 作为 negative result
- flat-NLI s44 + s45 eval 全部就绪 → 跑 plan_003 EDCR diagnostic（代码已在 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py`，14 方法 + 新增 2 个 seed）
- plan_003 完成 → 解锁 plan_002 test set eval（最终论文数据）
- reeval_rsft_topk3 待 GPU 空闲 → 提交（优先用 cuda:1，若 reeval_s43 占了则等下一个空闲 GPU）
- 所有核心 eval 收齐 → Paper Agent retitle + related work 扩展

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**:
- **C1 (evidence tax)**: SFT no-evidence rel_f1=0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106 = ~8pp cost; CED reranker 恢复到 0.4864 (97%)。已完整。
- **C2 (decoupling)**: QLoRA GRPO 导致 catastrophic evidence-extraction decoupling (rel_f1↑ but evi_f1↓); bf16 GRPO 不复现 → QLoRA-specific artifact。D054/D065 确认。
- **C3 (CED vs flat-NLI)**: 统计不可区分 → 降级，不作为独立贡献 (D056)。仍在收集额外 seeds。
- **论文定位**: "task definition + cost of faithfulness + decoupling finding + EDCR metric"

**易错点**:
- flat-NLI seed44/45 是**训练**，完成后还要跑**eval**才能用于 plan_003，别搞混
- reeval 系列是 D064 bug fix 重跑，和原始 eval_plan_001/008/012 同名但带 `_fixed` 或 `reeval_` 前缀，注意区分
- DPO 3ep 已确认过拟合 (rel_f1=0.3429)，1ep 是最后机会；如果 1ep 也崩 → DPO 全线作为 negative result 纳入论文 (D061)
- plan_003 EDCR diagnostic 代码已就绪（memory.md 有完整路径和运行命令），但需要所有 seed 的 predictions.json 才能跑
- EMNLP ARR July deadline ~7月15-25，剩余约 2 个月，P0 任务预估 ~30 GPU·h

```json
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
    ],
```

```json
{
  "parentUuid": "85c3cd31-692a-4711-8605-85223c6fc3e3",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me summarize:

**Worker outputs:**

1. **plan_011 analysis**: bf16 GRPO G=8 step eval results:
   - step_30: rel_f1=0.434, evi_f1=0.8063, edcr=0.6807
   - step_50: rel_f1=0.4276, evi_f1=0.8024, edcr=0.6855
   - step_70: rel_f1=0.4341, evi_f1=0.8099, edcr=0.6685

The metrics show a dip at step 50 before recovering at step 70, but all these early checkpoints still underperform compared to ckpt-100 which achieves rel_f1=0.4549. For the paper, I should report the ckpt-100 result as the bf16 GRPO performance since it's the strongest overall.

2. **re

I've submitted eval_flat_nli_s43 successfully on cuda:1 and it's loading the model now. The rel_f1=0.4549 I'm reporting comes from checkpoint-100 in the same run as plan_011, and the 0.0208 difference from step_70's 0.4341 is just from those extra 30 training steps.

On cuda:3, mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 crashed silently at 52% completion—likely an OOM error from trying to run two evaluations on the same GPU. Meanwhile eval_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 is still running at 50% with about an hour left.

Now I need to update the registry for reeval_flat_nli_s43 to mark it RUNNING and set up a cron job, finalize plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval with the metrics showing checkpoint-100 as the best performer, and investigate mvp2_eval_rsft_s44 since it appears to have been killed previously as well. eval_rsft_topk3 will need to wait until one of the running jobs finishes—likely eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 in about 58 minutes or reeval_flat_nli_s43 in 1-2 hours. I've got a few immediate tasks: updating plan_011 to DONE with its metrics, registering the cron job for reeval_flat_nli_s43, refreshing the report, and then reporting the results to the Director. Let me handle the registry updates first before reaching out.

For plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval, the analysis shows the bf16 GRPO training is improving overall despite non-monotonic dynamics, and critically, there's no task decoupling across any checkpoint—the evidence F1 stays consistently around 0.80-0.81. This confirms that the decoupling issue is specific to QLoRA, not GRPO itself, which is exactly what we needed to validate. Step-100 is the best performer at 0.4549 rel_f1, so I'm marking this as a success and updating the registry accordingly.

I'm also noticing that reeval_flat_nli_s43 shows PREPARING status even though submit_training_job should have updated it—I'll need to check if that status change got properly reflected.
```

_source line 99 · 2026-05-15T23:01:32.267Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01BEJnCXbipgjDeB51Ra7VPz`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

````json
  "task_prompt": "验证 plan_003 EDCR diagnostic 的准备度。\n\n## 背景\nplan_003 EDCR cross-method diagnostic table 是 P0 任务。代码在 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py`。它需要所有方法的 predictions.json 才能运行。\n\n## 任务\nSSH（read-only）到 rtx5090-4x 做以下检查：\n\n1. **验证脚本存在且可用**:\n   ```bash\n   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py\n   head -50 /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py\n   python /root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py --help 2>&1 | head -20\n   ```\n\n2. **盘点已有的 predictions.json**:\n   ```bash\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/ -name \"predictions.json\" -o -name \"metrics.json\" | sort\n   ```\n   \n3. **盘点需要但缺失的 predictions**:\n   当前还在跑或未跑的 eval：\n   - reeval_flat_nli_s43 (running, cuda:1) — flat-NLI seed=43 的 reeval\n   - flat-NLI seed=44 和 seed=45 — 训练中，完成后还要跑 eval\n   - reeval_rsft_topk3 — PREPARING，等 GPU\n   - eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 (running, cuda:3) — DPO 1ep eval\n   \n   检查哪些已经有 predictions.json，哪些还缺。\n\n4. **检查 EDCR 脚本期望的输入格式**:\n   看脚本的 argparse 或 config，确认它需要什么路径和参数。\n\n## 产出\n返回一个清单：\n- ✅ 已就绪的 predictions（方法名 + 路径）\n- ❌ 缺失的 predictions（方法名 + 依赖哪个实验完成）\n- 脚本运行命令模板\n- 预计何时可以运行（基于最晚的依赖实验）",

  ]
}
````
